whether it is compiled, whether it is open for editing, and cross
reference information.

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE REM_ALRT_PKG AS
G_Lng_No PLS_INTEGER;
G_Usr_No PLS_INTEGER;
G_CMP_NO PLS_INTEGER;
G_BRN_NO PLS_INTEGER;
G_BRN_USR PLS_INTEGER;
G_BRN_YEAR PLS_INTEGER;
G_GET_CNTRCT_ALRT PLS_INTEGER;
G_SEND_TYP_CNTRCT_ALRT PLS_INTEGER;
G_DAY_SEND_CNTRCT_ALRT PLS_INTEGER;
G_MSG_TXT_CNTRCT VARCHAR2(4000);
G_GET_BATCH_ALRT PLS_INTEGER;
G_SEND_TYP_BATCH_ALRT PLS_INTEGER;
G_DAY_SEND_BATCH_ALRT PLS_INTEGER;
G_MSG_TXT_BATCH VARCHAR2(4000);
G_GET_NOTE_ALRT PLS_INTEGER;
G_SEND_TYP_NOTE_ALRT PLS_INTEGER;
G_MSG_TXT_NOTE VARCHAR2(4000);
PROCEDURE GET_PRMTR ( P_Lng_no In Pls_Integer DEFAULT NULL,
P_Usr_No In Pls_Integer DEFAULT NULL,
P_CMP_NO IN NUMBER DEFAULT NULL,
P_BRN_NO IN NUMBER DEFAULT NULL);
PROCEDURE CHK_CNTRCT_ALRT;
PROCEDURE CHK_BATCH_ALRT;
PROCEDURE CHK_NOTE_ALRT (P_Doc_Srl Number,P_C_Phone Customer.C_Phone%Type,P_C_E_Mail Customer.C_E_Mail%Type);
End REM_ALRT_PKG;
NUMR$
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY REM_ALRT_PKG AS
--##--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
PROCEDURE GET_PRMTR ( P_Lng_no In Pls_Integer DEFAULT NULL ,
P_Usr_No In Pls_Integer DEFAULT NULL ,
P_BRN_NO IN NUMBER DEFAULT NULL
) Is
V_SCHEMA VARCHAR2(50);
SELECT SYS_CONTEXT('USERENV','CURRENT_SCHEMA')
INTO V_SCHEMA
FROM DUAL;
G_BRN_YEAR := SUBSTR(V_SCHEMA,4,4);
G_BRN_USR := SUBSTR(V_SCHEMA,8);
SELECT NVL(GET_CNTRCT_ALRT,0),NVL(SEND_TYP_CNTRCT_ALRT,1),NVL(DAY_SEND_CNTRCT_ALRT,0),MSG_TXT_CNTRCT,
NVL(GET_BATCH_ALRT,0), NVL(SEND_TYP_BATCH_ALRT,1), NVL(DAY_SEND_BATCH_ALRT,0),MSG_TXT_BATCH,
NVL(GET_NOTE_ALRT,0),NVL(SEND_TYP_NOTE_ALRT,1),MSG_TXT_NOTE
INTO
G_GET_CNTRCT_ALRT,G_SEND_TYP_CNTRCT_ALRT,G_DAY_SEND_CNTRCT_ALRT,G_MSG_TXT_CNTRCT,
G_GET_BATCH_ALRT,G_SEND_TYP_BATCH_ALRT,G_DAY_SEND_BATCH_ALRT,G_MSG_TXT_BATCH,
G_GET_NOTE_ALRT,G_SEND_TYP_NOTE_ALRT,G_MSG_TXT_NOTE
FROM REM_ALRT_PRMTR
WHERE ROWNUM<=1;
EXCEPTION WH
EN OTHERS THEN
NULL;
END;
If P_Lng_No IS NULL THEN
SELECT LANG_NO INTO G_Lng_No FROM IAS_SYS.LANG_DEF WHERE LANG_DFLT = 1 AND ROWNUM<=1;
G_Lng_No:=1;
G_Lng_No := P_Lng_no;
END IF;
G_Usr_No := P_Usr_No;
G_CMP_NO := P_Cmp_No;
G_BRN_NO := P_BRN_NO;
Raise_application_error(-20603,Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(G_Lng_No,670)||SqlErrm) ;
End GET_PRMTR;
PROCEDURE CHK_CNTRCT_ALRT IS
V_Msg_Txt
Varchar2(4000);
GET_PRMTR;
IF G_GET_CNTRCT_ALRT = 1 THEN -- USE CHK_CNTRCT_ALRT
Declare
Cursor Cntrct_Data Is Select Mst.Doc_Srl,Mst.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Mst.Cntrct_Amt,Mst.Cur_Code,Mst.T_Date_Ad,Cstm.C_Phone,Cstm.C_E_Mail
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Mst Mst,Customer Cstm
Where Mst.Scnd_Prty =Cstm.C_Code
And Rntl_Typ=1 And Nvl(Cncl_Rnt,0)=0
And To_Date(T_Date_Ad,'Dd/Mm/Yyyy')-To_Date(Sysdate,'Dd/Mm/Yyyy')=G_Day_Send_Cntrct_Alrt
Order By Mst.Doc_No;
For I in Cntrct_Data Loop
Execute Immediate G_Msg_Txt_Cntrct||' Where Rownum<=1 And Doc_Srl ='||I.Doc_Srl Into V_Msg_Txt ;
Exception When Others Then
Null;
IAS_SMS_MAIL_PKG.INSRT_MSG_ALRT_PRC (P_LNG_NO
=> G_Lng_NO,
P_MSG_MTHD_SEND
=> G_Send_Typ_Cntrct_Alrt,
P_MSG_TYP
=> 70,
P_MSG_TXT
=> V_MSG_TXT,
P_MOBILE_NO
=> I.C_Phone,
P_E_MAIL
=> I.C_E_Mail,
P_SUBJCT_MAIL
=> NULL,
P_DOC_TYP => 500,
P_DOC_SRL
=> I.DOC_SRL,
P_AD_DATE
=> SYSDATE,
P_CMP_NO
=> G_CMP_NO,
P_BRN_NO
=> G_BRN_NO,
P_BRN_USR
=> G_BRN_USR,
P_BRN_YEAR
=> G_BRN_YEAR );
COMMIT;
when others then
End Loop;
End If; -- USE CHK_CNTRCT_ALRT
END CHK_CNTRCT_ALRT;
PROCEDURE CHK_BATCH_ALRT IS
IF G_GET_BATCH_ALRT = 1 THEN -- USE CHK_BATCH_ALRT
Cursor Batch_Data Is Select Btch.Doc_Srl,Btch.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Btch.Batch_Amt,Btch.Rnt_Batch_No,Btch.Due_Date,Cstm.C_Phone,Cstm.C_E_Mail
,To_Date(Btch.Due_Date,'Dd/Mm/Yyyy')-To_Date(Sysdate,'Dd/Mm/Yyyy')
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Mst Mst,Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Btch Btch ,Customer Cstm
Where Mst.Doc_Srl=Btch.Doc_Srl
And Mst.Scnd_Prty =Cstm.C_Code
And Rntl_Typ=1
And To_Date(Btch.Due_Date,'Dd/Mm/Yyyy')-To_Date(Sysdate,'Dd/Mm/Yyyy')=G_Day_Send_Batch_Alrt
For I in Batch_Data Loop
Execute Immediate G_Msg_Txt_Batch||' Where Rownum<=1 And Doc_Srl = '||I.Doc_Srl||' And Rnt_Batch_No= '||I.Rnt_Batch_No Into V_Msg_Txt;
=> G_Send_Typ_Batch_Alrt,
--COMMIT;
End If; -- USE CHK_BATCH_ALRT
END CHK_BATCH_ALRT;
PROCEDURE CHK_NOTE_ALRT (P_Doc_Srl Number,P_C_Phone Customer.C_Phone%Type,P_C_E_Mail Customer.C_E_Mail%Type) IS
V_Msg_Txt Varchar2(4000);
IF G_GET_NOTE_ALRT = 1 THEN -- USE CHK_NOTE_ALRT
Execute Immediate G_Msg_Txt_Note||' Where Rownum<=1 And Doc_Srl ='||P_Doc_Srl Into V_Msg_Txt;
=> G_Send_Typ_Note_Alrt,
=> P_C_Phone,
=> P_C_E_Mail,
P_DOC_TYP => 514,
=> P_DOC_SRL,
End If; -- USE CHK_NOTE_ALRT
END CHK_NOTE_ALRT;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE REM_DOC_SQ as
FUNCTION Get_Doc_Typ_Serial (P_DOC_TYP In NUMBER) RETURN NUMBER ;
FUNCTIO
N Get_Doc_No ( P_Doc_Typ In Number,
P_Doc_Srl_Typ In Number DEFAULT NULL,
P_Brn_Year In Number,
P_Brn_No In S_BRN.BRN_NO%TYPE,
P_Typ_No In Number DEFAULT NULL,
P_Doc_Flg In Number DEFAULT NULL ) RETURN NUMBER ;
FUNCTION Get_Doc_Srl ( P_Doc_Typ In Number,
P_Doc_Flg In Number DEFAULT NULL,
P_Doc_No In Number ) RETURN NUMBER ;
End REM_DOC_SQ;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY REM_DOC_SQ as
FUNCTION Get_Doc_Typ_Serial (P_DOC_TYP In NUMBER) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_FLD_NM VARCHAR2(60);
V_DOC_SERIAL NUMBER;
/*-------------------------------------------
500 --
501 --
502 --
503 --
504 --
505 --
506 --
514 --
522--
-------------------------------------------*/
IF P_DOC_TYP IN(500,501,502,503,514,522,504,505,506)THEN
V_FLD_NM:= 'NVL(RENTAL_SERIAL,3)';
EXECUTE IMMEDIATE ('SELECT '||V_FLD_NM||' FROM REM_PARA ') INTO V_DOC_SERIAL;
RETURN (V_DOC_SERIAL);
RETURN(NULL);
--##-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
FUNCTION Get_Doc_No ( P_Doc_Typ In Number,
P_Doc_Flg In Number DEFAULT NULL ) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_Doc_Srl_Typ Number := P_Doc_Srl_Typ;
V_Doc_No Number;
If P_Doc_Typ Is Not Null And V_Doc_Srl_Typ Is Null Then
V_Doc_Srl_Typ:= Get_Doc_Typ_Serial (P_DOC_TYP => P_Doc_Typ );
If V_Doc_Srl_Typ Is Null Then
Raise_Application_Error (-20001,'Error When Select Serial Typ For Document , '||Sqlerrm);
End If;
If P_Doc_Typ =
500 Then
If V_Doc_Srl_Typ = 1 Then
Select nvl(Max(doc_no),0) + 1 Into V_Doc_No
From REM_CNTRCT_RNT_MST
Where Brn_No = P_Brn_No;
If V_Doc_Srl_Typ = 2 Then
where RNTL_TYP = P_Typ_No
and Brn_No = P_Brn_No;
If V_Doc_Srl_Typ = 3 Then
where Brn_No = P_Brn_No;
If V_Doc_Srl_Typ = 4 Then
where Brn_No = P_Brn_No
And RNTL_TYP = P_Typ_No;
If P_Doc_Typ = 501 Then
From REM_CNTRCT_RNT_CNCL_MST
If P_Doc_Typ = 502 Then
From REM_PRMTING_PRIOD_MST
If P_Doc_Typ = 503 Then
From REM_BILL_MST
End if;
If P_Doc_Typ = 504 Then
From Rem_Cntrct_Sale_Mst
And Pay_TYP = P_Typ_No;
If P_Doc_Typ = 505 Then
From Rem_Cntrct_Purchs_Mst
If P_Doc_Typ = 506 Then
Select Nvl(Max(doc_no),0) + 1 Into V_Doc_No
From Rem_Rq_Sale_Mst
If P_Doc_Typ = 514 Then
From REM_NOTE
WHERE Brn_No = P_Brn_No;
If P_Doc_Typ = 522 Then
From REM_REQ_MAINTEN
Select nvl(Max(doc_no)
,0) + 1 Into V_Doc_No
Return(V_Doc_No);
Return(null);
END Get_Doc_No;
P_Doc_No In Number ) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_Doc_Srl Number;
If P_Doc_Typ Is Not Null Then
If P_Doc_Typ =500 Then
If V_Doc_Srl_Typ = 1 Then -- Accumlated
V_Doc_Srl := P_Brn_Year||lpad(P_Doc_Typ,5,0)||P_Doc_No;
Elsif V_Doc_Srl_Typ = 2 Then -- - Serial By rental_type
V_Doc_Srl := P_Brn_Year||lpad(P_Doc_Typ,5,0)||lpad(P_Typ_No,5,0)||P_Doc_No;
Elsif V_Doc_Srl_Typ = 3 Then -- - Serial By Branch
V_Doc_Srl := P_Brn_Year||lpad(P_Brn_no,6,0)||lpad(P_Doc_Typ,5,0)||P_Doc_No;
Elsif V_Doc_Srl_Typ = 4 Then -- - Serial By Branch + Type_rental
V_Doc_Srl := P_Brn_Year||lpad(P_Brn_no,6,0)||lpad(P_Doc_Typ,5,0)||lpad(P_Typ_No,5,0)||P_Doc_No;
If P_Doc_Typ =501 Then
Elsif V_Doc_Srl_T
yp = 3 Then -- - Serial By Branch
If P_Doc_Typ =502 Then
If P_Doc_Typ =503 Then
If P_Doc_Typ =504 Then
If P_Doc_Typ =505 Then
If P_Doc_Typ =506 Then
If P_Doc_Typ =514 Then
If P_Doc_Typ =522 Then
Return (V_Doc_Srl );
Else
Return (Null );
--Return (Null );
Raise_Application_Error (-20002,'Error When Select Serial Typ For Document , '||Sqlerrm);
END Get_Doc_Srl;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE REM_GEN_PKG as
FUNCTION Get_Srvc_Name(P_Srvc_No In REM_CODE_SRVC.SRVC_NO%TYPE,P_Lng_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION Get_Ownr_Name(P_Ownr_No In REM_OWNRS.OWNR_NO%TYPE,P_Lng_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION Get_Real_Name(P_Real_No In REM_REAL_MST.REM_NO%TYPE,P_Lng_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION Get_Date_Ah(P_Date Date,p_frmt_typ number Default 1) RETURN VARCHAR2;
FUNCTION CHECK_LOCK (p_tab varchar2,p_whr varchar) RETURN Boolean ;
END REM_Gen_Pkg;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY REM_GEN_PKG as
FUNCTION Get_Srvc_Name(P_Srvc_No In REM_CODE_SRVC.SRVC_NO%TYPE,P_Lng_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
v_name varchar2(60);
select Decode(P_Lng_no,1,Srvc_L_Nm,nvl(Srvc_F_Nm,Srvc_L_Nm))
Into v_name
where Srvc_No=P_Srvc_No ;
RETURN(v_name);
RETURN(Null);
End Get_Srvc_Name ;
FUNCTION Get_Ownr_Name(P_Ownr_No In REM_OWNRS.OWNR_NO%TYPE,P_Lng_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
select Decode(P_Lng_
no,1,Ownr_L_Nm,nvl(Ownr_F_Nm,Ownr_L_Nm))
From REM_OWNRS
where Ownr_No=P_Ownr_No ;
End Get_Ownr_Name ;
FUNCTION Get_Real_Name(P_Real_No In REM_REAL_MST.REM_NO%TYPE,P_Lng_no In Number) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
v_name varchar2(90);
select Decode(P_Lng_no,1,Rem_L_Nm,nvl(Rem_F_Nm,Rem_L_Nm))
From REM_REAL_MST
where Rem_No=P_Real_No ;
End Get_Real_Name ;
FUNCTION Get_Date_Ah(P_Date Date,p_frmt_typ number Default 1 ) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
V_Date_Ah Varchar2(10);
If p_frmt_typ=1 Then
Select To_Char(P_DATE,'dd/mm/yyyy',' NLS_CALENDAR=''Arabic Hijrah''') Into V_Date_Ah
From dual;
Elsif p_frmt_typ=2 Then
Select To_Char(P_DATE,'ddmmyyyy',' NLS_CALENDAR=''Arabic Hijrah''') Into V_Date_Ah
Return v_Date_Ah;
When others Then
RETURN ' ';
End Get_Date_Ah;
FUNCTION CHECK_LOCK (p_tab varchar2,p_whr varchar) RETURN Boolean IS
row_locked EXCEPTION;
PRAGMA EXCEPTION_INIT(row_locked, -54);
V_Count Number:=0;
EXECUTE IMMEDIATE ('SELECT 1 FROM '||p_tab ||' where 1=1 AND ROWNUM <=1 '||p_whr ||' FOR UPDATE NOWAIT ') INTO V_Count;
Return False;
EXCEPTION
WHEN row_locked THEN
Return True;
END CHECK_LOCK;

V_LANG_NO PLS_INTEGER;
V_EX_RATE_F NUMBER;
G_Lng_No Pls_Integer;
G_Usr_No Pls_Integer;
G_loc_cur_Code EX_RATE.CUR_CODE%TYPE;
G_Pst_Date DATE;
G_Use_Audit_Doc Number;
G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once Number:=0;
G_TYP_POST_INCOME_CNCL Number:=0;

PROCEDURE Get_Prmtr_Prc ( P_Lng_no In Pls_Integer Default 1,
P_Usr_No In Pls_Integer);
FUNCTION Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc ( P_Doc_Typ In IAS_POST_MST.DOC_TYPE%TYPE , P_Doc_Srl In IAS_POST_MST.DOC_SER%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER ;
PROCEDURE Del_Doc_Prc ( P_Doc_Typ In IAS_POST_MST.DOC_TYPE%TYPE , P_Doc_Srl In IAS_POST_MST.DOC_SER%TYPE) ;

FUNCTION Get_Intrfc_Code (P_Fld_Nm In Varchar2,P_Brn_No In Number,P_Msg_NO Number Default Null ) RETURN VARCHAR2;

,P_F_Doc_No In NUMBER Default Null
,P_T_Doc_No In NUMBER Default Null
,P_F_Doc_Date In DATE Default Null
,P_T_Doc_Date In DATE Default Null
,P_Doc_Srl In NUMBER Default Null
,P_Doc_Jv_Typ In NUMBER Default Null
,P_Brn_No In S_BRN.BRN_NO%TYPE Default Null );

PROCEDURE Pst_Cntrct_Cncl_Prc ( P_Pst_Typ In NUMBER Default 0
,P_Brn_No In S_BRN.BRN_NO%TYPE Default Null ) ;
PROCEDURE Pst_Bill_Service_Prc ( P_Pst_Typ In NUMBER Default 0
PROCEDURE Pst_Cntrct_Sale_Prc ( P_Pst_Typ In NUMBER Default 0
PROCEDURE Pst_Cntrct_Purchs_Prc ( P_Pst_Typ In NUMBER Default 0
END REM_POSTING_PKG;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY REM_POSTING_PKG AS
P_Usr_No In Pls_Integer) Is
G_Lng_No:=P_Lng_no;
G_Usr_No:=P_Usr_No;
Select Cur_Code Into G_loc_cur_code from Ex_Rate Where L_F=1;
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20420,'Error When select Local Cur. from Ex_Rate , '||SQLERRM);
Select Pst_Cust_Trns_Once Into G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once from Rem_Para Where Rownum<=1;
G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once:=0;
Select Nvl(TYP_POST_INCOME_CNCL,1) Into G_TYP_POST_INCOME_CNCL from Rem_Para Where Rownum<=1;
G_TYP_POST_INCOME_CNCL:=1;
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20420,'Error When select Gnr_Prmtr , '||SQLERRM);
End Get_Prmtr_Prc;
FUNCTION Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc ( P_Doc_Typ In IAS_POST_MST.DOC_
TYPE%TYPE , P_Doc_Srl In IAS_POST_MST.DOC_SER%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_cnt NUMBER:=0;
Select 1
Into V_cnt
From IAS_POST_MST
Where DOC_TYPE = P_Doc_Typ
And DOC_SER = P_Doc_Srl
And RowNum<=1;
RETURN (V_cnt);
WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
RETURN (0);
WHEN OTHERS THEN
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR (-20442,'Error When Select IAS_POST_MST '||SqlErrm);
END Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc;
PROCEDURE Del_Doc_Prc ( P_Doc_Typ In IAS_POST_MST.DOC_TYPE%TYPE , P_Doc_Srl In IAS_POST_MST.DOC_SER%TYPE) IS
DELETE FROM IAS_POST_DTL
WHERE DOC_TYPE = P_Doc_Typ
AND DOC_SER = P_Doc_Srl;
DELETE FROM IAS_POST_MST
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR (-20443,'Error When Delete IAS_POST_MST '||SqlErrm);
END Del_Doc_Prc;
P_Lng_no In Pls_Integer) Is
IAS_POSTING_PKG.GET_PARA(P_LANG_NO => P_Lng_no, P_USER_NO=> P_Usr_No);
--.Get_Prmtr_Prc (P_Lng_no=>P_Lng_no , P_Usr_No=>P_Usr_No);
If Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc ( P_Doc_Typ => P_Doc_Typ , P_Doc_Srl=> P_Doc_Srl ) > 0 Then
Del_Doc_Prc ( P_Doc_Typ => P_Doc_Typ , P_Doc_Srl=> P_Doc_Srl ) ;
Get_Prmtr_Prc (P_Lng_no=>P_Lng_no , P_Usr_No=>P_Usr_No);
If P_Doc_Typ = 500 Then
Pst_Cntrct_Prc (P_Pst_Typ =>0,P_Doc_Srl=>P_Doc_Srl);
ElsIf P_Doc_Typ = 501 Then
Pst_Prmting_Priod_Prc (P_Pst_Typ =>0,P_Doc_Srl=>P_Doc_Srl);
ElsIf P_Doc_Typ = 502 Then
Pst_Cntrct_Cncl_Prc (P_Pst_Typ =>0,P_Doc_Srl=>P_Doc_Srl);
ElsIf P_Doc_Typ = 503 Then
Pst_Bill_Service_Prc (P_Pst_Typ =>0,P_Doc_Srl=>P_Doc_Srl);
ElsIf P_Doc_Typ = 504 Then
Pst_Cntrct_Sale_Prc (P_Pst_Typ =>0,P_Doc_Srl=>P_Doc_Srl);
ElsIf P_Doc_Typ = 505 Then
Pst_Cntrct_Purchs_Prc (P_Pst_Typ =>0,P_Doc_Srl=>P_Doc_Srl);
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20420,'Error When Insert Doc. Into Posting Tables , '||SQLERRM);
END Insrt_Doc_In_Pst_Prc;
FUNCTION Get_Intrfc_Code (P_Fld_Nm In Varchar2, P_Brn_No In Number,P_Msg_NO Number Default Null ) RETURN Varchar2 Is
v_a_code ACCOUNT.A_CODE%TYPE;
If P_Fld_Nm Is Not Null and P_Brn_No Is Not Null Then
Execute Immediate ('Select '||P_Fld_Nm ||' From Interface_Acc Where Brn_no = '||P_Brn_No) Into v_a_code ;
--Select REM_A_CODE_INCOM_DFRD Into v_a_code From Interface_Acc Where Brn_no = P_Brn_No ;
If v_a_code Is Not Null Then
Return v_a_code;
Raise_application_error(-20605,'Interfaca Acc, '||Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(V_Lang_No,Nvl(P_Msg_No,1904))||SqlErrm) ;
end if;
End Get_Intrfc_Code;
PROCEDURE Pst_Cntrct_Prc(P_Pst_Typ In NUMBER Default 0
,P_Brn_No In S_BRN.BRN_NO%TYPE Default Null ) Is
V_Dtl_Insrt Number;
V_Doc_Dsc Varchar2(250);
V_Rcrd_No Number:=0;
V_Curr_Blnc Number;
V_A_Code_Ret Varchar2(30);
V_C_Code_Ret Varchar2(30);
V_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd Varchar2(30);
V_Cnt_Mnth Number;
V_Amt_Mnth Number;
V_F_Date Date;
V_T_Date Date;
V_Amt_Day Number:=0;
V_Rnt_Amt Number;
V_Count_Day Number:=0;
V_Count_Day_Mnth Number:=0;
V_Ac_Dtl_Typ Numbe
r:=0;
V_Typ_Post_Income Number:=0;
V_Conn_Post_Rep_Code Number:=0;
V_A_Code_Rep_Code Varchar2(99);
V_Doc_Date_Mst Date;
For M_cv In
( Select 500 Doc_Typ
,1 Jv_Typ
,Mst.doc_no Doc_no
,Mst.doc_srl Doc_srl
,Mst.F_date_ad Doc_date
,Mst.F_date_ad F_Date_Ad
,Mst.T_date_ad T_Date_Ad
,Mst.ref_no Ref_no
,Mst.Doc_Dsc Doc_Dsc
,Mst.Doc_Dsc_Insurnc Doc_Dsc_Insurnc
,0 Extrnl_Pst
,Mst.Cntrct_Amt Cntrct_Amt
,(Nvl(Mst.Cntrct_Amt,0)) Amt_L
,Decode(Mst.Cur_Code,G_Loc_Cur_Code,0,Nvl(Mst.Cntrct_Amt,0)) Amt_F
,Mst.cc_code cc_code
,Mst.pj_no pj_no
,Mst.actv_no actv_no
,Mst.cur_code cur_code
,Mst.Ex_Rate Ex_Rate
,Mst.FRST_PRTY Ownr_No
,Mst.SCND_PRTY c_code
,Mst.UNPOST_U_ID UNPOST_U_ID
,Mst.UNPOST_date UNPOST_DATE
,Mst.Ad_U_Id Ad_U_Id
,Mst.Ad_date AD_date
,Mst.Up_U_Id Up_U_Id
,Mst.Up_date Up_date
,Mst.Up_cnt Up_cnt
,Mst.PR_REP Prnt_cnt
,Mst.Brn_No Brn_No
,Mst.Cmp_No Cmp_No
,Mst.Brn_Year Brn_Year
,Mst.Brn_Usr Brn_Usr
,Mst.Audit_Ref
,Mst.Audit_Ref_Desc
,Mst.Audit_Ref_U_Id
,Mst.Audit_Ref_Date
,Mst.AMT_INSURNC AMT_INSURNC
,Mst.PAY_MTHD PAY_MTHD
,Mst.CASH_NO Cash_no
,Mst.A_CODE_MRKTR A_CODE_MRKTR
,Mst.COMM_MRKTR_RT COMM_MRKTR_RT
,Mst.COMM_MRKTR_PRTY COMM_MRKTR_PRTY
,Mst.A_CODE_OFFICE A_CODE_OFFICE
,Mst.COMM_OFFICE_RT COMM_OFFICE_RT
,Mst.COMM_OFFICE_PRTY COMM_OFFICE_PRTY
,Mst.Rem_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd Rem_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd
,Mst.C_Code Cst_Code
,Mst.Rep_Code
,Mst.COMM_REP_CODE_RT
,Mst.COMM_REP_CODE_PRTY
,Mst.A_CODE_INSURANCE
,Mst.CNCL_RNT
From REM_CNTRCT_RNT_MST Mst
Where nvl(Mst.Doc_Post,0)= Decode(Nvl(P_Pst_Typ,0),0,0,nvl(Mst.Doc_Post,0))
and Mst.doc_no between nvl(P_f_doc_no,Mst.doc_no) and nvl(P_t_doc_no,Mst.doc_n
and Mst.doc_date between nvl(P_f_doc_date,Mst.doc_date) and nvl(P_t_doc_date,Mst.doc_date)
and Mst.doc_srl = nvl(P_doc_srl, Mst.doc_srl)
and Mst.Brn_No = nvl(P_Brn_No,Mst.Brn_No)
and Exists (Select 1 from S_BRN_USR_PRIV Prv
Where Prv.U_Id = G_Usr_No
and Prv.Brn_No = Mst.Brn_No
and Prv.Add_Flag = 1
and RowNum<=1)
For Update NoWait)
Loop --(loop Mst)--
If G_TYP_POST_INCOME_CNCL=2 AND NVL(M_cv.CNCL_RNT,0)=1 Then
V_Doc_Post Number:=0;
V_Doc_No Number:=0;
Select Nvl(Doc_Post,0),Doc_No Into V_Doc_Post,V_Doc_No
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Cncl_Mst
Where Doc_Srl_Cncl=M_Cv.Doc_Srl;
V_Doc_Post:=0;
If V_Doc_Post=1 Then
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20420,Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(G_Lng_No,3165)||' '||V_Doc_No);
IF M_cv.Cur_code=G_loc_cur_Code Then
v_ex_rate_f:=0;
v_ex_rate_f:=1;
If Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc (P_Doc_Typ => M_Cv.Doc_Typ, P_Doc_Srl => M_Cv.Doc_Srl ) = 0 Then -- (Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc)--
V_Count:=V_Count+1;
If M_Cv.Doc_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then
V_Doc_Date_Mst:=M_Cv.Doc_Date;
V_Doc_Date_Mst:=Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day;
Ias_Posting_Pkg.Set_Doc_Info (
P_Audit_Ref => M_Cv.Audit_Ref ,
P_Audit_Ref_Desc => M_Cv.Audit_Ref_Desc ,
P_Audit_Ref_U_Id => M_Cv.Audit_Ref_U_Id ,
P_Audit_Ref_Date => M_Cv.Audit_Ref_Date ,
P_Ad_U_Id => M_Cv.Ad_U_Id ,
P_Ad_Date => M_Cv.Ad_Date ,
P_Up_U_Id => M_Cv.Up_U_Id ,
P_Up_Date => M_Cv.Up_Date ,
P_Up_Cnt => M_Cv.Up_Cnt ,
P_Unpost_U_Id => M_Cv.Unpost_U_Id ,
P_Unpost_Date => M_Cv.Unpost_Date ,
P_Brn_No => M_Cv.Brn_No ,
P_Brn_Year => M_Cv.Brn_Year ,
P_Cmp_No => M_Cv.Cmp_No ,
P_Brn_Usr => M_Cv.Brn_Usr ,
P_Post_Type => P_Pst_Typ );
Ias_Posting_Pkg.Insrt_Post_Mst(
P_Doc_Type => M_Cv.Doc_Typ ,
P_Jv_Type => M_Cv.Jv_Typ ,
P_Doc_No => M_Cv.Doc_No ,
P_Doc_Ser => M_Cv.Doc_Srl ,
P_J_Date => V_Doc_Date_Mst ,
P_Ref_No => M_Cv.Ref_No );
-- ## Start Batch Journal ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--V_Dtl_Insrt:=0;
V_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd:=M_Cv.Rem_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd;
If G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once=1 Then -- G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once--
If M_Cv.Doc_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd --
V_Doc_Dsc := Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,3424)||' '||M_Cv.Doc_No||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,280)||' '||M_Cv.F_Date_Ad||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,253)||' '||M_Cv.T_Date_Ad;
--## Account (Dr Side)--
V_A_Code_Ret:=Ias_Cst_Pkg.Get_Acode(M_Cv.C_Code);
V_Curr_Blnc:=Ias_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (V_A_Code_Ret,M_Cv.Cur_Code);
Ias_Posting_Pkg.Insrt_Post_Dtl(
P_A_Code => Ias_Cst_Pkg.Get_Acode(M_Cv.C_Code),
P_C_Code => M_Cv.C_Code,
P_Ac_Code_Dtl => M_Cv.C_Code,
P_Ac_Dtl_Typ => 3,
P_A_Cy => M_Cv.Cur_Code ,
P_Amt => (M_Cv.Amt_L)* Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1) ,
P_Amt_F => (M_Cv.Amt_F)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
P_J_Date => M_Cv.Doc_Date ,
P_J_Desc => M_Cv.Doc_Dsc ,
P_Cc_Code => M_Cv.Cc_Code ,
P_Pj_No => M_Cv.Pj_No ,
P_Actv_No => M_Cv.Actv_No ,
P_Cb_Before => V_Curr_Blnc ,
P_Ref_No => M_Cv.Ref_No ,
P_Rcrd_No => V_Count ,
P_Brn_No => M_Cv.Brn_No,
P_Doc_Brn_No => M_Cv.Brn_No);
--## Account (Cr Side)--
V_Curr_Blnc:=Ias_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (V_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd,M_Cv.Cur_Code);
P_A_Code => V_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd ,
P_Amt => -(M_Cv.Amt_L )* Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1) ,
P_Amt_F => -(M_Cv.Amt_F)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
P_J_Desc => V_Doc_Dsc ,
P_Brn_No => M_Cv.Brn_No,--Nvl(D_Cv.Doc_Brn_No,M_Cv.Brn_No),
P_Doc_Brn_No => M_Cv.Brn_No);--D_Cv.Doc_Brn_No );
V_Dtl_Insrt:=1;
End If; -- if prd --
Else -- G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once--
For D_Cv In
( Select Dtl.Rnt_Batch_No Batch_No
,Dtl.F_Date_Ad Doc_Date
,F_Date_Ad F_Date_Ad
,T_Date_Ad T_Date_Ad
,F_Date_Ah F_Date_Ah
,T_Date_Ah T_Date_Ah
,(Nvl(Dtl.Batch_Amt,0)) Amt_L
,Decode(M_Cv.Cur_Code,G_Loc_Cur_Code,0,Nvl(Dtl.Batch_Amt,0)) Amt_F
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Btch Dtl
Where Dtl.Doc_Srl = M_Cv.Doc_Srl)
Loop --(3)--
V_Doc_Dsc := Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,3424)||' '||M_Cv.Doc_No||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.G
et_Prompt(G_Lng_No,1304)||' '||D_Cv.Batch_No||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,280)||' '||D_Cv.F_Date_Ad||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,253)||' '||D_Cv.T_Date_Ad;
--## Account (Dr Side)-
If D_Cv.Doc_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd (2) --
P_Amt => ( D_Cv.Amt_L)* Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1) ,
P_Amt_F => ( D_Cv.Amt_F)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
P_J_Date => D_Cv.Doc_Date ,
--## Account (Cr Side) --
P_Amt => -(D_Cv.Amt_L )* Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1) ,
P_Amt_F => -(D_Cv.Amt_F)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
P_J_D
ate => D_Cv.Doc_Date ,
End If; --if prd (2) --
End Loop; --(3) --
End If; -- G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once--
-- ## End Batch Journal ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ## Start Service Journal ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
( Select A_Code_Rvnu, (Select Srvc_L_Nm From Rem_Code_Srvc C Where C.Srvc_No=Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Srvc.Srvc_No) Nm_Srvc,Amt_Srvc,
Decode(M_Cv.Cur_Code,G_Loc_Cur_Code,0,Nvl(Amt_Srvc,0)) Amt_F,Amt_Srvc Amt_L
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Srvc
Where Doc_Srl=M_Cv.Doc_Srl)
Loop -- Loop Service --(1)
V_Doc_Dsc := Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,606)||' '||D_Cv.Nm_Srvc||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,3424)||' '||M_Cv.Doc_No;
IF Nvl(D_Cv.Amt_L,0)<>0 Then -- if service 1
If M_Cv.Doc_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd (3) --
--## Account (Dr Side)
P_A_Code => D_Cv.A_Code_Rvnu ,
P_Doc_Brn_No => M_Cv.Brn_No );
End If; -- If Prd (2) --
End If;-- if service 1
End Loop; -- loop service (1)
( Select Rnt_Batch_No,Decode(M_Cv.Cur_Code,G_Loc_Cur_Code,0,Nvl(Amt_L,0)) Amt_F,A_Code_Rvnu,Doc_Date,Amt_L,Nm_Srvc
From ( Select Rnt_Batch_No,Amt_Srvc/(Select Count(Rnt_Batch_No)From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Btch Bin Where Bin.Doc_Srl=B.Doc_Srl) Amt_L ,Due_Date Doc_Date,A_Code_Rvnu
,(Select Srvc_L_Nm From Rem_Code_Srvc C Where C.Srvc_No=S.Srvc_No) Nm_Srvc
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Btch B,Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Srvc S
Where B.Doc_Srl=S.Doc_Srl And S.Doc_Srl= M_Cv.Doc_Srl))
Loop -- Loop Service (2)
If D_Cv.Doc_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd (3) --
Ias_Posting_Pkg.Ins
rt_Post_Dtl(
P_A_Code
=> D_Cv.A_Code_Rvnu ,
End Loop; -- loop service (2)
End If; -- if Service
-- ## End Service Journal ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ## Start Insurance Journal------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
If Nvl(M_Cv.Amt_Insurnc,0) > 0 Then -- if Insurance 1 --
--V_Doc_Dsc := Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,360)||' '|| Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,3424) ||M_cv.Doc_no||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,388)||M_cv.Cst_Code;
If M_Cv.Pay_Mthd In (1,2) Then -- if Mthd --
V_A_Code_Ret:=Ias_Cshbnk_Pkg.Get_A_Code(M_Cv.Pay_Mthd,M_Cv.Cash_No);
--## Account (Dr Side) --
P_A_Code => V_A_Code_Ret,
P_Amt => ((Nvl(M_Cv.Amt_Insurnc,0) * Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1))),
P_Amt_F => (M_Cv.Amt_Insurnc)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
P_Cash_No => M_Cv.Cash_No,
P_Ac_Code_Dtl => M_Cv.Cash_No,
P_Ac_Dtl_Typ => 1,
P_J_Desc => M_Cv.Doc_Dsc_Insurnc ,
Elsif M_Cv.Pay_Mthd=4 Then -- if Mthd --
V_A_Code_Ret:=Ias_Cst_Pkg.Get_Acode(M_Cv.Cst_Code);
P_A_Code => Ias_Cst_Pkg.Get_Acode(M_Cv.Cst_Code),
P_C_Code => M_Cv.Cst_Code,
P_Ac_Code_Dtl => M_Cv.Cst_Code,
End If; -- if Mthd --
If M_Cv.A_Code_Insurance Is Not Null T
V_A_Code_Ret:=M_Cv.A_Code_Insurance;
V_A_Code_Ret:=Get_Intrfc_Code('Rem_A_Code_Insurance',M_Cv.Brn_No,1903);
P_Amt => -((Nvl
(M_Cv.Amt_Insurnc,0) * Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1))),
P_Amt_F => -( M_Cv.Amt_Insurnc )* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
End If;--Id Prd (3) --
End If; -- if Insurance 1 --
-- ## End Insurance Journal------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ## Start Comm. Journals ( By Marketer)----------------------------------------------------------------------------
If M_Cv.Doc_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd (4) --
V_Ac_Dtl_Typ:=0;
If M_Cv.A_Code_Mrktr Is Not Null And Nvl(M_Cv.Comm_Mrktr_Rt,0)>0 Then -- if comm Market --
If Nvl(M_Cv.Comm_Mrktr_Prty,0)=1 Then
V_A_Code_Ret :=Null;
Select A_Code Into V_A_Code_Ret From Rem_Ownrs Where Ownr_No=M_Cv.Ownr_No;
V_C_Code_Ret:=Null;
Raise_Application_Error(-20606,'Rem_Ownrs, '||Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(V_Lang_No,1902)||Sqlerrm) ;
Elsif Nvl(M_Cv.Comm_Mrktr_Prty,0)=2 Then
V_C_Code_Ret:=M_Cv.C_Code;
V_Ac_Dtl_Typ:=3;
V_Do
c_Dsc :=Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,5197)||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,3424)||' '||M_Cv.Doc_No;
IAS_Posting_Pkg.Insrt_Post_Dtl(
p_doc_type => M_cv.doc_typ ,
p_jv_type => M_cv.jv_typ ,
p_doc_no => M_cv.doc_no ,
p_doc_ser => M_cv.doc_srl ,
p_a_code => v_a_code_Ret,
P_C_CODE => V_C_code_Ret,
P_AC_CODE_DTL => V_C_code_Ret,
P_AC_DTL_TYP => V_AC_DTL_TYP,
p_a_cy => M_cv.cur_code ,
p_amt => ((M_cv.CNTRCT_AMT/100*M_cv.COMM_MRKTR_RT)*(nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1))),
p_amt_f => ((M_cv.CNTRCT_AMT/100*M_cv.COMM_MRKTR_RT)*(nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0))),
p_j_date => M_cv.DOC_date ,
p_j_desc => v_doc_dsc ,
p_cc_code => M_cv.cc_code ,
p_pj_no => M_cv.pj_no ,
p_Actv_No => M_cv.Actv_No ,
p_cb_before => v_curr_blnc ,
p_ref_no => M_cv.ref_no ,
p_rcrd_no => v_count ,
--## Account (Cr Side)
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (M_cv.A_CODE_MRKTR,M_cv.cur_code);
v_count:=v_count+1;
p_a_code => M_cv.A_CODE_MRKTR,
p_amt => -((M_cv.CNTRCT_AMT/100*M_cv.COMM_MRKTR_RT)*(nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1))),
p_amt_f => -(M_cv.CNTRCT_AMT/100*M_cv.COMM_MRKTR_RT)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0),
p_j_desc => v_
doc_dsc,
END IF; -- if comm Market --
End If; -- if prd (4) --
-- ## End Comm. Journals ( By Marketer)----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ## Start Comm. Journals ( By Office)-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
V_AC_DTL_TYP:=0;
If M_Cv.A_Code_Office Is Not Null And Nvl(M_Cv.Comm_Office_Rt,0)>0 Then -- if comm Office --
If M_Cv.Doc_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd (5) --
If Nvl(M_Cv.Comm_Office_Prty,0)=1 Then
Elsif Nvl(M_Cv.Comm_Office_Prty,0)=2 Then
V_Doc_Dsc :=Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,5198)||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,3424)||' '||M_cv.Doc_No;
P_C_Code => V_C_Code_Ret,
P_Ac_Code_Dtl => V_C_Code_Ret,
P_Ac_Dtl_Typ => V_Ac_Dtl_Typ,
P_Amt => ((M_Cv.Cntrct_Amt/100*M_Cv.Comm_Office_Rt)*(Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1))),
P_Amt_F => (M_Cv.Cntrct_Amt/100*M_Cv.Comm_Office_Rt)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0),
P_J_Desc => V_Doc_Dsc,
V_Curr_Blnc:=Ias_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (M_Cv.A_Code_Office,M_Cv.Cur_Code);
P_A_Code => M_Cv.A_Code_Office,
P_Amt => -((M_Cv.Cntrct_Amt/100*M_Cv.Comm_Office_Rt)*(Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1))),
P_Amt_F => -(M_Cv.Cntrct_Amt/100*M_Cv.Comm_Office_Rt)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0),
End If ;-- if Prd (5) --
End If; -- if comm Office --
-- ## End Comm. Journals ( By Office)-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ## Start Comm. Journals ( By Rep_code )-------------------------------------------------------------------------
Select Nvl(Conn_Mark_Rep_Code,0) Into V_Conn_Post_Rep_Code
From Rem_Para Where Rownum<=1;
Excepti
Select Comm_A_Code Into V_A_Code_Rep_Code
From Interface_Acc Where Brn_No=M_Cv.Brn_No And Rownum<=1;
If M_Cv.Doc_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd (6) --
If V_Conn_Post_Rep_Code=1 And M_Cv.Comm_Rep_Code_Prty=2 Then -- If Rep_Code --
If M_Cv.Rep_Code Is Not Null And M_Cv.Comm_Rep_Code_Rt Is Not Null Then
V_Doc_dsc :=Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,1964)||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,3424)||' '||M_cv.Doc_No;
P_Amt => ((M_Cv.Cntrct_Amt/100*M_Cv.Comm_Rep_Code_Rt)*(Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1))),
P_Amt_F => ((M_Cv.Cntrct_Amt/100*M_Cv.Comm_Rep_Code_Rt)*(Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0))),
V_Curr_Blnc:=Ias_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (V_A_Code_Rep_Code,M_Cv.Cur_Code);
P_A_Code => V_A_Code_Rep_Code,
P_Amt => -((M_Cv.Cntrct_Amt/100*M_Cv.Comm_Rep_Code_Rt)*(Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1))),
P_Amt_F => -(M_Cv.Cntrct_Amt/100*M_Cv.Comm_Rep_Code_Rt)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0),
End If; -- If Rep_Code --
End If; --If Prd (6) --
-- ## End Comm. Journals ( By Rep_code )-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ## Start Revenue. Journals ( Revenue ) --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Select Nvl(Typ_Post_Income,0) Into V_Typ_Post_Income From Rem_Para;
If Nvl(V_Typ_Post_Income,0)=0 Then -- If Type Post Day (1) --
V_Cnt_Mnth:=Months_Between(To_Date(To_Char(M_Cv.T_Date_Ad,'Mm/Yyyy'),'Mm/Yyyy'),To_Date( To_Char(M_Cv.F_Date_Ad,'Mm/Yyyy'),'Mm/Yyyy'))+1;
V_Count_Day:=Trunc((M_Cv.T_Date_Ad-M_Cv.F_Date_Ad)+1);
For D_Cv2 In
( Select Dtl.Rem_No Rem_No
,Dtl.Rnt_Amt Rnt_Amt
,Dtl.A_Code A_Code
,Dtl.Cc_Code Cc_Code
,Dtl.Pj_No Pj_No
,Dtl.Actv_No Actv_No
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Dtl Dtl
Loop --(4) --
V_F_Date:=M_Cv.F_Date_Ad;
V_Amt_Day:=D_Cv2.Rnt_Amt/V_Count_Day;
For Rec In 1..V_Cnt_Mnth Loop --(5) --
If Last_Day(V_F_Date)>=M_Cv.T_Date_Ad Then
V_Count_Day_Mnth:=(M_Cv.T_Date_Ad-V_F_Date)+1;
V_Count_Day_Mnth:=(Last_Day(V_F_Date)-V_F_Date)+1;
If V_F_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd (7) --
V_Amt_Mnth:=V_Count_Day_Mnth*V_Amt_Day;
V_Doc_Dsc :=Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,8339)||' '||D_cv2.rem_no||' '|| Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,5387) ||' '||To_Char(V_F_date,'mm')||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,3424)||' '||M_cv.Doc_No;
P_Amt => V_Amt_Mnth*Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1),
P_Amt_F => V_Amt_Mnth * Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
P_J_Date => V_F_Date ,
P_Cc_Code => D_Cv2.Cc_Code ,
P_Pj_No => D_Cv2.Pj_No ,
P_Actv_No => D_Cv2.Actv_No ,
P_Install_Type => 2,
V_Curr_Blnc:=Ias_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (D_Cv2.A_Code,M_Cv.Cur_Code);
P_A_Code => D_Cv2.A_Code,
P_Amt => -(V_Amt_Mnth*Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1)),
P_Amt_F => -(V_Amt_Mnth) * Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
End If; -- If Prd (7)--
IF Last_Day(V_F_Date)>=M_Cv.T_Date_Ad Then
V_F_Date:=M_Cv.T_Date_Ad;
V_F_Date:=Last_Day(V_F_Date)+1;
End Loop;--(5) --
End Loop; --(4)--
Elsif Nvl(V_Typ_Post_Income,0)=1 Then -- Type Post Same Batch
,Btch.Batch_Amt/100*(Dtl.Rnt_Amt/M_Cv.Cntrct_Amt*100) Amt
,Due_Date Doc_Date
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Dtl Dtl ,Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Btch Btch
Where Dtl.Doc_Srl =Btch.Doc_Srl
And Dtl.Doc_Srl = M_Cv.Doc_Srl )
Loop --(6) --
If D_Cv2.Doc_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd (8) --
P_Doc_No => M_Cv.Doc_No
P_Amt => D_Cv2.Amt * Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1),
P_Amt_F => D_Cv2.Amt * Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
P_J_Date => D_Cv2.Doc_Date ,
P_Amt => -(D_Cv2.Amt*Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1)),
P_Amt_F => -(D_Cv2.Amt* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0)) ,
End If; --If Prd (8) --
End Loop;--(6)
End If; -- If Type Post Day (1) --
-- ## End Revenue. Journals ( Revenue ) --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
If V_Dtl_Insrt = 0 Then
Raise_application_error(-20610,'Error When Insert Data Into Ias_Post_Dtl Null Data ' ||SqlErrm) ;
END IF; -- (Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc)--
--## Check Balance
Ias_Posting_Pkg.Update_IntrM_Tab ( P_doc_type => M_cv.doc_typ ,
P_doc_ser => M_cv.doc_srl ,
P_jv_type => M_cv.jv_typ ,
P_doc_no => M_cv.doc_no ,
P_doc_date => M_cv.doc_date ,
P_local_cur => null ,
P_Lang_no => G_Lng_No ,
P_cc_code => Null ,
P_PJ_NO => Null ,
P_Actv_NO => Null ,
P_ref_no => M_cv.ref_no ,
p_ad_u_id => M_cv.ad_u_id ,
p_post_u_id => G_Usr_No ,
P_Post_Type => P_Pst_Typ ,
P_Brn_no => M_cv.Brn_no ,
P_Brn_Year => M_cv.Brn_Year ,
P_CMP_NO => M_cv.CMP_NO ,
P_BRN_USR => M_cv.BRN_USR );
--## Update Post flag
If P_Pst_Typ = 1 Then -- Posting Case
G_Pst_Date:= SysDate;
Update REM_CNTRCT_RNT_MST Set Doc_post = 1 ,
POST_U_ID = G_Usr_no,
POST_DATE = G_Pst_Date
Where Doc_Srl = M_cv.Doc_Srl;
Raise_application_error(-20613,'Prl Trust., '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(G_Lng_No,0)||SqlErrm) ;
If Sql%NotFound Then
Raise_application_error(-20614,'Prl Trust., '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(G_Lng_No,0)||SqlErrm) ;
End Loop;--(loop Mst)--
END Pst_Cntrct_Prc;
PROCEDURE Pst_Prmting_Priod_Prc(P_Pst_Typ In NUMBER Default 0
V_Dtl_Insrt NUMBER;
V_Doc_Dsc VARCHAR2(250);
V_Rcrd_No NUMBER:=0;
v_curr_blnc NUMBER;
v_count NUMBER:=0;
v_a_code_Ret VARCHAR2(30);
v_c_code_ret VARCHAR2(30);
V_A_CODE_DISCOUNT VARCHAR2(30);
( Select 501 Doc_Typ
,Mst.DOC_SRL_REF DOC_SRL_REF
,Mst.DOC_NO_REF DOC_NO_REF
,Mst.doc_date Doc_date
,Mst.CNTRCT_AMT CNTRCT_AMT
,Mst.Audit_Ref Audit_Ref
,Mst.Audit_Ref_Desc Audit_Ref_Desc
,Mst.Audit_Ref_U_Id Audit_Ref_U_Id
,Mst.Audit_Ref_Date Audit_Ref_Date
,Mst.Days_Cntrct Days_Cntrct
From REM_PRMTING_PRIOD_MST Mst
and Mst.doc_no between nvl(P_f_doc_no,Mst.doc_no) and nvl(P_t_doc_no,Mst.doc_no)
Loop --(6)
IF Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc (P_DOC_TYP => M_cv.Doc_Typ, P_DOC_SRL => M_cv.Doc_Srl ) = 0 THEN -- (3)
--##----------------------------------------------------------------##--
IAS_Posting_Pkg.Set_Doc_Info (
P_Audit_Ref => M_cv.Audit_Ref ,
P_Audit_Ref_Desc => M_cv.Audit_Ref_Desc ,
P_Audit_Ref_U_Id => M_cv.Audit_Ref_U_Id ,
P_Audit_Ref_Date => M_cv.Audit_Ref_Date ,
P_Ad_U_Id => M_cv.Ad_U_Id ,
P_Ad_Date => M_cv.Ad_Date ,
P_Up_U_Id => M_cv.Up_U_Id ,
P_Up_Date => M_cv.Up_Date ,
P_Up_Cnt => M_cv.Up_Cnt ,
P_Unpost_U_Id => M_cv.Unpost_U_Id ,
P_Unpost_Date => M_cv.Unpost_Date ,
P_Brn_No => M_cv.brn_no ,
P_Brn_Year => M_cv.brn_year ,
P_BRN_USR => M_cv.BRN_USR ,
IAS_Posting_Pkg.Insrt_Post_Mst(
p_jv_type => M_cv.Jv_Typ,
p_j_date => M_cv.doc_date ,
p_ref_no => M_cv.ref_no );
V_Dtl_Insrt:=0;
V_A_CODE_DISCOUNT:=Get_Intrfc_Code('REM_A_CODE_DISCOUNT',M_cv.Brn_No,1904);
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (V_A_CODE_DISCOUNT,M_cv.cur_
code);
For D_cv3 In
,Dtl.Day_Count Day_Count
,Dtl.Day_Rvnu Day_Rvnu
,Dtl.F_DATE_AD F_DATE_AD
,Dtl.T_DATE_AD T_DATE_AD
,Dtl.F_DATE_Ah F_DATE_Ah
,Dtl.T_DATE_Ah T_DATE_Ah
,Dtl.Amt_Due Amt
From Rem_Prmting_Priod_Dtl Dtl
Loop --(7)
v_curr_blnc:
=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (V_A_CODE_DISCOUNT,M_cv.cur_code);
V_Rcrd_No:=V_Rcrd_No+1;
P_DOC_SER_REF => M_cv.DOC_SRL_REF,
P_DOC_NO_REF => M_cv.DOC_NO_REF,
p_a_code => V_A_CODE_DISCOUNT ,
p_amt => D_cv3.Amt*nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1),
p_amt_f => D_cv3.Amt* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0) ,
p_j_desc => M_Cv.doc_dsc ,
p_cc_code => D_cv3.cc_code ,
p_pj_no => D_cv3.pj_no ,
p_Actv_No => D_cv3.Actv_No ,
p_rcrd_no => V_Rcrd_No ,
v_a_code_ret:=IAS_CST_PKG.GET_ACODE(M_cv.c_code);
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (v_a_code_ret,M_cv.cur_code);
p_jv_type => M_cv.Jv_typ ,
p_a_code => v_a_code_ret,
P_C_CODE => M_cv.c_code,
P_AC_CODE_DTL => M_cv.c_code,
P_AC_DTL_TYP => 3,
p_amt => -(D_cv3.amt)* nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1) ,
p_amt_f => -(D_cv3.amt)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0) ,
p_j_desc => M_cv.doc_dsc ,
End Loop;--(7)
END IF; --(3)
-- If V_Dtl_Insrt = 0 Then
-- Raise_application_error(-20610,'Error When Insert Data Into Ias_Post_Dtl Null Data ' ||SqlErrm) ;
--End If;
Update REM_PRMTING_PRIOD_MST Set Doc_post = 1 ,
END Pst_Prmting_Priod_Prc;
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Procedure Pst_Cntrct_Cncl_Prc ( P_Pst_Typ In Number Default 0
,P_F_Doc_No In Number Default Null
,P_T_Doc_No In Number Default Null
,P_F_Doc_Date In Date Default Null
,P_T_Doc_Date In Date Default Null
,P_Doc_Srl In Number Default Null
,P_Doc_Jv_Typ In Number Default Null
,P_Brn_No In S_Brn.Brn_No%Type Default Null ) Is
V_Amt Number;
V_Typ_Post_Income_
Cncl Number:=1;
V_Amt_Diff Number:=0;
For M_Cv In
( Select 502 Doc_Typ
,Mst.Doc_No Doc_No
,Mst.Doc_Srl Doc_Srl
,Mst.Doc_Srl_Cncl Doc_Srl_Cncl
,Mst.Doc_No_Cncl Doc_No_Cncl
,Mst.Doc_Date Doc_Date
,Mst.Doc_Date F_Date_Ad
,Mst.T_Date_Ad T_Date_Ad
,Mst.Ref_No Ref_No
,Mst.Amt_Diff Amt_Diff
,Mst.A_Code_Diff A_Code_Diff
,Mst.Cc_Code Cc_Code
,Mst.Pj_No Pj_No
,Mst.Actv_No Actv_No
,Mst.Cur_Code Cur_Code
,Mst.Frst_Prty Ownr_No
,Mst.Scnd_Prty C_Code
,Mst.Unpost_U_Id Unpost_U_Id
,Mst.Unpost_Date Unpost_Date
,Mst.Ad_Date Ad_Date
,Mst.Up_Date Up_Date
,Mst.Up_Cnt Up_Cnt
,Mst.Pr_Rep Prnt_Cnt
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Cncl_Mst Mst
Where Nvl(Mst.Doc_Post,0)= Decode(Nvl(P_Pst_Typ,0),0,0,Nvl(Mst.Doc_Post,0))
And Mst.Doc_No Between Nvl(P_F_Doc_No,Mst.Doc_No) And Nvl(P_T_Doc_No,Mst.Doc_No)
And Mst.Doc_Date Between Nvl(P_F_Doc_Date,Mst.Doc_Date) And Nvl(P_T_Doc_Date,Mst.Doc_Date)
And Mst.Doc_Srl = Nvl(P_Doc_Srl, Mst.Doc_Srl)
And Mst.Brn_No = Nvl(P_Brn_No,Mst.Brn_No)
And Exists (Select 1 From S_Brn_Usr_Priv Prv
And Prv.Brn_No = Mst.Brn_No
And Prv.Add_Flag = 1
And Rownum<=1)
For Update Nowait)
Loop -- Loop Mst --
If G_Typ_Post_Income_Cncl=2 Then
V_Doc_Post Number;
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Mst
Where Doc_Srl=M_Cv.Doc_Srl_Cncl
And Rownum<=1;
If V_Doc_Post=0 Then
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20420,Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(G_Lng_No,3164)||' '||V_Doc_No);
If M_Cv.Cur_Code=G_Loc_Cur_Code Then
V_Ex_Rate_F:=0;
V_Ex_Rate_F:=1;
If Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc (P_Doc_Typ => M_Cv.Doc_Typ, P_Doc_Srl => M_Cv.Doc_Srl ) = 0 Then -- If Check Doc --
P_Jv_Type => M_Cv.Jv_Typ,
--( Start Batch Journal )------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
Select Nvl(Typ_Post_Income,0) Into V_Typ_Post_Income From Rem_Para
Where Rownum<=1;
V_Typ_Post_Income:=0;
Select Rem_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd Into V_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd
Where Doc_Srl=M_Cv.Doc_Srl_Cncl;
Raise_Application_Error(-20606,' Not Found A_Code_Incom_Dfrd . '||Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(V_Lang_No,1902)||Sqlerrm) ;
If G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once=1 Then -- G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once (1) --
If V_Typ_Post_Income=1 Then -- If Post Icom --
V_T_Date:=M_Cv.F_Date_Ad;
V_Amt_Mnth:=0;
( Select Btch.Batch_Amt Amt
,Btch.F_Date_Ad F_Date_Ad
,Btch.T_Date_Ad T_Date_Ad
,Abs(T_Date_Ad-F_Date_Ad)+1 Count_Days
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Btch Btch
Where Btch.Doc_Srl = M_Cv.Doc_Srl_Cncl
And M_Cv.F_Date_Ad >= Btch.F_Date_Ad And M_Cv.F_Date_Ad <=Btch.T_Date_Ad)
Loop
V_F_Date :=D_Cv2.F_Date_Ad;
V_Count_Day_Mnth:=Abs(D_Cv2.T_Date_Ad-M_Cv.F_Date_Ad)+1;
V_Amt_Day:=D_Cv2.Amt/D_Cv2.Count_Days;
V_Amt_Mnth:=V_Amt_Mnth+(V_Count_Day_Mnth*V_Amt_Day);
V_T_Date:=D_Cv2.T_Date_Ad;
Select Nvl(Sum(Batch_Amt),0) Into V_Amt
And Btch.F_Date_Ad >=V_T_Date;
V_Amt:=Nvl(V_Amt,0)+Nvl(V_Amt_Mnth,0);
V_Doc_Dsc:=Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,8341)||' - '||V_Count_Day;
P_Doc_Ser_Ref => M_Cv.Doc_Srl_Cncl,
P_Doc_No_Ref => M_Cv.Doc_No_Cncl,
P_A_Code => V_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd,
P_Amt => (V_Amt)* Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1) ,
P_Amt_F => (V_Amt)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
P_J_Dat
e => M_Cv.Doc_Date ,
V_Curr_Blnc :=Ias_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (V_A_Code_Ret,M_Cv.Cur_Code);
P_C_Code => M_Cv.C_Code ,
P_Ac_Code_Dtl => M_Cv.C_Code ,
P_Ac_Dtl_Typ => 3 ,
P_Amt => -(Abs(V_Amt-Nvl(M_Cv.Amt_Diff,0)))* Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1) ,
P_Amt_F => -(Abs(V_Amt-Nvl(M_Cv.Amt_Diff,0)))* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
--## Amt _Diff
If M_Cv.Amt_Diff Is Not Null Then -- If Amt Diff --
P_A_Code => M_Cv.A_Code_Diff,
P_Amt => -(M_Cv.Amt_Diff)* Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1) ,
P_Amt_F => -(M_Cv.Amt_Diff)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
P_J_Desc => Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,145)||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,999),
End If; -- If Amt Diff --
Else -- If Post Icom --
V_Count_Day:=Abs(M_Cv.T_Date_Ad-M_Cv.Doc_Date)+1;
V_Amt:=V_Count_Day*(M_Cv.Cntrct_Amt/M_Cv.Days_Cntrct);
P_Amt_F => (V_Amt)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0)
Ias_Posting_Pkg.Insrt_Pos
t_Dtl(
P_J_Desc => Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,145)||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,999) ,
End If; -- If Post Icome
Else -- G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once --
-- G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Batch
V_F_Date :=M_Cv.F_Date_Ad;
Where ( Btch.Doc_Srl = M_Cv.Doc_Srl_Cncl
Or (Btch.Doc_Srl = M_Cv.Doc_Srl_Cncl And Btch.F_Date_Ad>=M_Cv.F_Date_Ad )
Order by Rnt_Batch_No )
If M_Cv.F_Date_Ad>D_Cv2.F_Date_Ad Then
V_T_Date:=D_Cv2.F_Date_Ad;
V_Doc_Dsc := Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,8341)||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,3424)||' '||M_Cv.Doc_No_Cncl||' '||
Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,280)||' '||V_T_Date||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,253)||' '||D_Cv2.T_Date_Ad;
--V_F_Date :=D_Cv2.F_Date_Ad;
V_Count_Day_Mnth:=Abs(D_Cv2.T_Date_Ad-V_T_Date)+1;
V_Amt_Mnth:=(V_Count_Day_Mnth*V_Amt_Day);
V_Amt:=Nvl(V_Amt_Mnth,0);
-- V_T_Date:=D_Cv2.T_Date_Ad;
If V_T_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd --
P_J_Date => V_T_Date ,
P_Amt => -(Abs(V_Amt))* Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1) ,
P_Amt_F => -(Abs(V_Amt))* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
End If; -- if Prd --
End Loop ; -- D_Cv2
For D_Cv3 In ( Select Btch.Batch_Amt Amt,
Btch.F_Date_Ad F_Date_Ad,
Btch.T_Date_Ad T_Date_Ad,
Abs(T_Date_Ad-F_Date_Ad)+1 Count_Days
And Btch.F_Date_Ad >=V_T_Date)
loop -- d_cv3
V_Amt:=D_Cv3.Amt;
Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,280) ||' '||D_Cv3.F_Date_Ad||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,253)||' '||D_Cv3.T_Date_Ad;
If D_Cv3.F_Date_Ad Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd --
P_Amt => (V_Amt)* Nvl(M_
Cv.Ex_Rate,1) ,
P_J_Date => D_Cv3.F_Date_Ad ,
End If; -- iif Prd --
End Loop ; -- D_Cv3
-- End If; -- if Prd --
End If; -- G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once --
--( End Batch Journal )------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
--( Start Service. Journals)------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
For D_Cv3 In ( Select Srvc.Amt_Srvc Amt
,Srvc.Srvc_No Srvc_No
,Srvc.A_Code_Rvnu A_Code_Rvnu
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Srvc Srvc
Where Srvc.Doc_Srl = M_Cv.Doc_Srl_Cncl )
Loop -- Loop Service
V_Curr_Blnc:=Ias_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (D_Cv3.A_Code_Rvnu ,M_Cv.Cur_Code);
V_Amt:=V_Count_Day*(D_Cv3.Amt/M_Cv.Days_Cntrct);
V_Doc_Dsc:=Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,152)||' '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,5202);
P_A_Code => D_Cv3.A_Code_Rvnu ,
P_Rcrd_No
=> V_Count ,
--V_Doc_Dsc:=;
P_Amt => -(V_Amt)* Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1) ,
P_Amt_F => -(V_Amt)* Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
End If ; -- If Prd (1) --
End Loop; -- Loop Service
V_T_Date:=Null;
For D_Cv3 In ( Select Srvc.Amt_Srvc/(Select Count(Rnt_Batch_No)From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Btch Bin Where Bin.Doc_Srl=Btch.Doc_Srl) Amt
,Btch.F_DATE_AD
,Btch.T_DA
TE_AD
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Btch Btch ,Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Srvc Srvc
Where Btch.Doc_Srl = Srvc.Doc_Srl And Srvc.Doc_Srl=M_Cv.Doc_Srl_Cncl
And Btch.T_Date_Ad >= M_Cv.F_Date_Ad
Order By Btch.Rnt_Batch_No)
If M_Cv.F_Date_Ad>D_Cv3.F_Date_Ad Then
V_T_Date:=D_Cv3.F_Date_Ad;
V_Count_Day:=Abs(D_Cv3.T_DATE_AD-V_T_Date)+1;
V_Amt:=V_Count_Day*(D_Cv3.Amt/Abs((D_Cv3.T_DATE_AD-D_Cv3.F_DATE_AD)+1));
End If; --G_Pst_Cust_Trns_Once (1) --
--( End Service. Journals)------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
--( Start Revenue. Journals)------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
Select Nvl(Typ_Post_Income_Cncl,1) Into V_Typ_Post_Income_Cncl From Rem_Para
V_Typ_Post_Income_Cncl:=1;
If Nvl(V_Typ_Post_Income_Cncl,0)=2 Then -- I
f Post Icome --
And Btch.Doc_Srl = M_Cv.Doc_Srl_Cncl
Loop -- Loop Batch Revc --
V_Doc_Dsc:=Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,8339)||' - '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Prompt(G_Lng_No,670);
If V_F_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd (2) --
P_Amt => (V_Amt_Mnth*Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1)),
P_Amt_F => (V_Amt_Mnth) * Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0) ,
P_Rcrd_No => V_Rcrd_No ,
P_Amt => -(V_Amt_Mnth * Nvl(M_Cv.Ex_Rate,1)),
P_Amt_F => -(V_Amt_Mnth * Nvl(V_Ex_Rate_F,0)) ,
End Loop; -- Loop Batch resv --
Delete Ias_Post_Dtl Where Doc_Ser=M_Cv.Doc_Srl_Cncl
And Install_Type=2 And Doc_Type=500 And Doc_Date>=M_Cv.F_Date_Ad;
Else -- Type Post Day
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Cncl_Dtl Dtl
Loop -- Loop Cncl Dtl --
V_Amt_Day:=D_Cv2.Rnt_Amt/M_Cv.Days_Cntrct;
For Rec In 1..V_Cnt_Mnth Loop -- Loop Mnth --
If V_F_Date Between Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day And Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Final_Day Then -- if Prd (3) --
V_Curr_Blnc:=Ias_Posting_Pkg.Check_
Acode (V_A_Code_Incom_Dfrd,M_Cv.Cur_Code);
End If; --If Prd (3) --
End Loop; -- Loop Mnth --
End Loop; -- Loop Cncl Dtl --
End If; -- If Post Icome --
Raise_Application_Error(-20610,'Error When Insert Data Into Ias_Post_Dtl Null Data ' ||Sqlerrm) ;
End If;-- If Check Doc --
Ias_Posting_Pkg.Update_Intrm_Tab ( P_Doc_Type => M_Cv.Doc_Typ ,
P_Doc_Date => M_Cv.Doc_Date ,
P_Local_Cur => Null ,
P_Lang_No => G_Lng_No ,
P_Cc_Code => Null ,
P_Pj_No => Null ,
P_Actv_No => Null ,
P_Post_U_Id => G_Usr_No ,
P_Brn_Usr => M_Cv.Brn_Usr );
--## Update Post Flag
G_Pst_Date:= Sysdate;
Update Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Cncl_Mst Set Doc_Post = 1 ,
Post_U_Id = G_Usr_No,
Post_Date = G_Pst_Date
Where Doc_Srl = M_Cv.Doc_Srl;
Raise_Application_Error(-20613,'Prl Trust., '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(G_Lng_No,0)||Sqlerrm) ;
If Sql%Notfound Then
Raise_Application_Error(-20614,'Prl Trust., '||Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(G_Lng_No,0)||Sqlerrm) ;
End Loop;-- Loop Mst --
End Pst_Cntrct_Cncl_Prc;

V_Cnt_Mnth

If Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc (P_Doc_Typ => M_Cv.Doc_Typ, P_Doc_Srl => M_Cv.Doc_Srl ) = 0 Then -- (2)

-----------------------------------------------------##--
--##------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--

Procedure Pst_Cntrct_Sale_Prc ( P_Pst_Typ In Number Default 0
REM_A_CODE_INCOM_SALE VARCHAR2(30);
V_Amt_Cash Number:=0;
V_AC_DTL_TYP Number:=0;
( Select 504 Doc_Typ
,Mst.PAY_typ PAY_Typ
,Mst.CSH_NO Cash_no
,Mst.A_CODE_CSH A_CODE_CSH
,Mst.BNK_NO Bank_No
,Mst.A_CODE_BNK A_CODE_BNK

,Mst.CHEQUE_DUE_DATE CHEQUE_DUE_DATE
,Mst.A_CODE_INCM_SALE A_CODE_INCM_SALE
From Rem_Cntrct_Sale_Mst Mst
and Mst.Brn_No
= nvl(P_Brn_No,Mst.Brn_No)
Loop --(1)
IF Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc (P_DOC_TYP => M_cv.Doc_Typ, P_DOC_SRL => M_cv.Doc_Srl ) = 0 THEN -- (2)
P_Up
_Cnt => M_cv.Up_Cnt ,

p_amt_f => -( M_cv.CNTRCT_AMT)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0) ,
p_j_date => M_cv.Doc_date ,
Elsif M_cv.Pay_Typ=2 Then
V_A_Code_Ret:=Ias_Cshbnk_Pkg.Get_A_Code(2,M_Cv.Bank_No);
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (M_cv.a_code_bnk,M_cv.cur_code);
p_a_code => M_cv.a_code_bnk ,
p_cash_no => M_cv.bank_no,
P_AC_CODE_DTL => M_cv.bank_no,
P_AC_DTL_TYP => 2,
Elsif M_cv.Pay_Typ=3 Then
--## Post Bank
p_amt => ((nvl(M_cv.CHEQUE_AMT,0) * nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1))),
p_amt_f => (M_cv.CHEQUE_AMT)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0) ,
p_amt => -((nvl(M_cv.CHEQUE_AMT,0) * nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1))),
p_amt_f => -( M_cv.CHEQUE_AMT)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0) ,
--## Post Hand_Cash
V_Amt_Cash:=abs(M_cv.CNTRCT_AMT-M_cv.CHEQUE_AMT);
p_amt => ((nvl(V_Amt_Cash,0) * nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1))),
p_amt_f => (V_Amt_Cash)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0) ,
p_amt => -((nvl(V_Amt_Cash,0) * nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1))),
p_amt_f => -( V_Amt_Cash)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0) ,
Elsif M_cv.Pay_Typ=4 Then
D_cv In
( Select Dtl.INSTL_NO INSTL_NO
,Dtl.DUE_DATE DUE_DATE
,Dtl.DUE_DATE_AH DUE_DATE_AH
,(nvl(Dtl.amt,0)) amt_l
,Decode(M_cv.cur_code,G_loc_cur_Code,0,nvl(Dtl.amt,0)) amt_f
From Rem_Cntrct_Instlmnt Dtl
Where Dtl.Doc_Srl = M_cv.Doc_Srl)
Loop --(3)
'||D_cv.INSTL_NO||'
'||D_cv.DUE_DATE;
p_a_code => IAS_CST_PKG.GET_ACODE(M_cv.c_code),
p_amt => ( D_cv.amt_l)* nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1) ,
p_amt_f => ( D_cv.amt_f)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0) ,
p_j_date => D_cv.Due_date ,
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (REM_A_CODE_INCOM_SALE,M_cv.cur_code);
p_a_code => REM_A_CODE_INCOM_SALE ,
p_amt => -(D_cv.amt_l )* nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1) ,
p_amt_f => -(D_cv.amt_f)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0) ,
End Loop; --(3)
End If; -- (3)
--##-------------------------
-------------------------------------
--##--
-- Comm. Journals ( By Marketer)
If M_cv.A_CODE_MRKTR Is Not Null And Nvl(M_cv.COMM_MRKTR_RT,0)>0 THEN
If Nvl(M_cv.COMM_MRKTR_PRTY,0)=1 Then
v_a_code_ret :=Null;
Select A_Code Into V_A_Code_Ret From REM_OWNRS Where Ownr_No=M_cv.Ownr_No;
V_C_code_Ret:=null;
Raise_application_error(-20606,'Rem_Ownrs, '||Ias_Gen_Pkg.Get_Msg(V_Lang_No,1902)||SqlErrm) ;
ElsIf Nvl(M_cv.COMM_MRKTR_PRTY,0)=2 Then
V_C_code_Ret:=M_cv.c_code;
V_AC_DTL_TYP:=3;
v_doc_dsc :='
'||M_cv.Doc_No;
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (v_a_code_Ret,M_cv.cur_code);
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting
_Pkg.Check_Acode (M_cv.A_CODE_MRKTR,M_cv.cur_code);
p_j_desc => M_cv.doc_dsc,
-- Comm. Journals ( By Office)
If M_cv.A_CODE_OFFICE Is Not Null And Nvl(M_cv.COMM_OFFICE_RT,0)>0 THEN
If Nvl(M_cv.COMM_OFFICE_PRTY,0)=1 Then
ElsIf Nvl(M_cv.COMM_OFFICE_PRTY,0)=2 Then
-## Account (Dr Side)
p_amt => ((M_cv.CNTRCT_AMT/100*M_cv.COMM_OFFICE_RT)*(nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1))),
p_amt_f => (M_cv.CNTRCT_AMT/100*M_cv.COMM_OFFICE_RT)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0),
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (M_cv.A_CODE_OFFICE,M_cv.cur_code);
p_a_code => M_cv.A_CODE_OFFICE,
p_amt => -((M_cv.CNTRCT_AMT/100*M_cv.COMM_OFFICE_RT)*(nvl(M_cv.ex_rate,1))),
p_amt_f => -(M_cv.CNTRCT_AMT/100*M_cv.COMM_OFFICE_RT)* nvl(v_ex_rate_f,0),
END IF; -- (2)
Raise_application_error(-20618,'Error When Insert Data Into Ias_Post_Dtl Null Data ' ||SqlErrm) ;
Update Rem_Cntrct_Sale_Mst Set Doc_post = 1 ,
End Pst_Cntrct_Sale_Prc;

CREATE OR REPLACE Package Rem_Fetch_Data_Pkg Is
Type t_cv Is Ref Cursor;
Procedure Get_Real_Trns( a_cv In Out t_cv,
P_Lang_No In NUMBER Default 1,
P_Whr In VARCHAR2,
P_OrdBy In VARCHAR2,
P_User_No In NUMBER,
P_ARALT In NUMBER,
P_APALT In NUMBER,
P_Trns_Typ In NUMBER,
P_Ac_Dtl_Typ In NUMBER DEFAULT 0,
P_Prd_Typ In NUMBER DEFAULT 0,
P_REM_NO In NUMBER);
Procedure Get_Sum_Blnc( a_cv In Out t_cv,
Procedure Rem_Customer ( a_cv In Out t_cv,
P_Whr In VARCHAR2);
Procedure Real_Trans ( A_cv In Out t_cv,
P_Whr In VARCHAR2 );
Procedure Cntrct_Trans ( A_cv In Out t_cv,
End Rem_Fetch_Data_Pkg;
CREATE OR REPLACE Package Body Rem_Fetch_Data_Pkg is
P_REM_NO In NUMBER) Is
Open a_cv For
' Select Null Prd_No,
Null Prd_Parent,
Pst.doc_no,
Pst.doc_date,
doc_type_name (:P_Lang_No,Pst.doc_type,Null) doc_type_nm,
Pst.a_cy,
Case
when nvl(Pst.dr_amt_f,0)=0 then Pst.dr_amt else Pst.dr_amt_f end dr_amt_acy,
Case when nvl(Pst.cr_amt_f,0)=0 then Pst.cr_amt else Pst.cr_amt_f end cr_amt_acy,
Pst.dr_amt dr_amt_l,
Pst.cr_amt cr_amt_l,
Pst.doc_desc,
Pst.ref_no,
Pst.cc_code,
Decode(:P_Lang_No,1,Nvl(Cc.Cc_A_Name,Cc.Cc_E_Name),Nvl(Cc.Cc_E_Name,Cc.Cc_A_Name)) Cc_Nm,
Pst.pj_no,
Decode(:P_Lang_No,1,Nvl(Pj.Pj_A_Name,Pj.Pj_E_Name),Nvl(Pj.Pj_E_Name,Pj.Pj_A_Name)) Pj_Nm,
Pst.actv_no,
Decode(:P_Lang_No,1,Nvl(Actv.Actv_A_Name,Actv.Actv_E_Name),Nvl(Actv.Actv_E_Name,Actv.Actv_A_Name)) Actv_Nm,
Pst.brn_no,
Decode(:P_Lang_No,1,Nvl(BRN.BRN_LNAME,BRN.BRN_FNAME),Nvl(BRN.BRN_FNAME,BRN.BRN_LNAME)) Brn_Nm,
Pst.Doc_Ser,
Pst.doc_type,
Pst.jv_type doc_jv_type,
Pst.Ad_U_Id,Pst.Ad_Date,Pst.Up_U_Id,Pst.Up_Date,Pst.Up_Cnt
From Ias_Post_Dtl Pst,Cost_Centers Cc,Ias_Projects Pj,Ias_Actvty Actv,S_Brn Brn
Where Pst.Cc_Code = Cc.Cc_Code(+)
And Pst.Pj_No = Pj.Pj_No(+)
And Pst.Actv_No = Actv.Actv_No(+)
And Pst.Brn_No = Brn.Brn_No
And Pst.doc_type IN (500,501,502)
and Exists ( Select 1 From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Dtl Rem
Where Rem.Doc_Srl=Pst.Doc_ser
And Rem.A_Code=Pst.A_Code
And Rem.Rem_no= :P_REM_NO
And RowNum<=1) '||P_Whr||' '||P_OrdBy
Using P_Lang_No,
P_Lang_No,
P_REM_NO;
Raise_application_error(-20200,'Error When Query From Real Esate Trans '|| chr(13) || SqlErrm);
End Get_Real_Trns;
--##------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
V_Whr varchar2(5000);
Open a_cv for
' Select Pst.a_cy,
Sum(Cas
e when nvl(Pst.dr_amt_f,0)=0 then Pst.dr_amt else Pst.dr_amt_f end) sum_dr_amt,
Sum(Case when nvl(Pst.cr_amt_f,0)=0 then Pst.cr_amt else Pst.cr_amt_f end) sum_cr_amt,
Sum((Case when nvl(Pst.dr_amt_f,0)=0 then Pst.dr_amt else Pst.dr_amt_f end)-
(Case when nvl(Pst.cr_amt_f,0)=0 then Pst.cr_amt else Pst.cr_amt_f end)) Blnc_Amt,
Sum(Pst.dr_amt) sum_dr_amt_l,
Sum(Pst.cr_amt) sum_cr_amt_l,
Sum(Pst.dr_amt-Pst.cr_amt) Blnc_Amt_l
And RowNum<=1) '||P_Whr||
'Group by Pst.a_cy '
USING
P_REM_NO ;
Raise_application_error(-20201,'Error When Query From Real Esate Trans '|| chr(13) || SqlErrm);
End Get_Sum_Blnc;
Procedure Rem_Customer ( A_cv In Out t_cv,
P_Whr In VARCHAR2 )Is
' Select Rem_No,Mst.Doc_No,Mst.F_Date_Ad,Mst.F_Date_Ah,Mst.T_Date_Ad,Mst.T_Date_Ah,Mst.Scnd_Prty,Rnt_Amt,Mst.cur_code,Mst.brn_no,Mst.Doc_dATE

Where Mst.Doc_Srl=Dtl.Doc_Srl'||' '||P_WHR;
Raise_application_error(-20200,'Error When Query From Rem_Customer '|| chr(13) || SqlErrm);
End Rem_Customer;
' SELECT DOC_NO,DOC_DATE,DOCTYPNAME,C_NAME,AMT,CUR_CODE,BRN_NO,REM_NO,CC_CODE,doc_type,c_code,Doc_Srl FROM (
Select Mst.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Doc_Type_Name(:P_Lang_No,500) Doctypname,Mst.cc_Code,
(Select Decode(:P_Lang_No,1,C_A_Name,C_E_Name) From Customer Where C_Code=Mst.Scnd_Prty)C_Name,Cntrct_Amt AMT,
Cur_Code,Mst.Brn_No,Dtl.Rem_No,500 doc_type,Mst.Scnd_Prty c_code,Mst.Doc_Srl
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Dtl Dtl,Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Mst Mst
Where Mst.Doc_Srl=Dtl.Doc_Srl
Union All
Select Mst.
Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Doc_Type_Name(:P_Lang_No,502) Doctypname,Mst.cc_Code,

Diff_Rvnu_Rntl Amt,Cur_Code,Mst.Brn_No,Dtl.Rem_No,502 Doc_Type,Mst.Scnd_Prty c_code,Mst.Doc_Srl
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Cncl_Dtl Dtl,Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Cncl_Mst Mst
Select Mst.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Doc_Type_Name(:P_Lang_No,501) Doctypname,Mst.cc_Code,(Select Decode(:P_Lang_No,1,
C_A_Name,C_E_Name) From Customer Where C_Code=Mst.Scnd_Prty)C_Name,AMT_DUE AMT,Cur_Code,Mst.Brn_No,
Dtl.Rem_No,501 Doc_Type,Mst.Scnd_Prty c_code,Mst.Doc_Srl
From Rem_Prmting_Priod_Dtl Dtl,Rem_Prmting_Priod_Mst Mst
Select Mst.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Doc_Type_Name(:P_Lang_No,504) Doctypname,Mst.cc_Code,(Select Decode(:P_Lang_No,1,
C_A_Name,C_E_Name) From Customer Where C_Code=Mst.Scnd_Prty)C_Name,Cntrct_Amt AMT,Cur_Code,Mst.Brn_No,Mst.Rem_No,
504 Doc_Type,Mst.Scnd_Prty c_code,Doc_Srl
Select Mst.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Doc_Type_Name(:P_Lang_No,505) Doctypname,Mst.cc_Code,(Select Decode(:P_Lang_No,1,
505 Doc_Type,Mst.Scnd_Prty c_code,Doc_Srl
From Rem_Cntrct_Purchs_Mst Mst) Real_Trans '||' '||P_Whr||' '||' Order By Doc_Date,Doc_No'
P_Lang_No ;

End Real_Trans;
Procedure Cntrct_Trans( A_cv In Out t_cv,
'Select doc_no
,doc_date
,doc_type_name(:P_Lang_No,doc_type,Null) docTypName
,doc_desc
,Case when nvl(amt,0)> 0 then amt else null end dr
,Case when nvl(amt,0)< 0 then abs(amt) else null end cr
,a_cy
,Brn_No
,doc_type
,doc_ser
From Ias_Post_Dtl
Where c_code Is Not Null '||p_whr
using
P_Lang_No;

End Cntrct_Trans;

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER IAS_VD_REM_TGR
BEFORE DELETE OR INSERT OR UPDATE
OF A_CY
,AC_AMT
,AC_AMTF
,AC_CODE_DTL
,CNTRCT_NO
,CNTRCT_SRL
,CNTRCT_BTCH
ON VOUCHER_DETAIL
REFERENCING NEW AS NEW OLD AS OLD
FOR EACH ROW
If DELETING Then --(1)
IF :OLD.VOUCHER_TYPE = 1 AND :OLD.CNTRCT_NO IS NOT NULL AND :OLD.CNTRCT_SRL IS NOT NULL AND :OLD.AC_DTL_TYP=3 AND :OLD.AC_CODE_DTL IS NOT NULL THEN --(2)
UPDATE REM_CNTRCT_RNT_BTCH SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0)- DECODE(NVL(:OLD.AC_AMTF,0),0,ABS(:OLD.AC_AMT),ABS(:OLD.AC_AMTF))
WHERE DOC_NO = :OLD.CNTRCT_NO
AND DOC_SRL = :OLD.CNTRCT_SRL
AND RNT_BATCH_NO = :OLD.CNTRCT_BTCH;
ElsIf INSERTING Then --(1)
IF :NEW.VOUCHER_TYPE = 1 AND :NEW.CNTRCT_NO IS NOT NULL AND :NEW.CNTRCT_SRL IS NOT NULL AND :NEW.AC_DTL_TYP=3 AND :NEW.AC_CODE_DTL IS NOT NULL THEN --(2)
UPDATE REM_CNTRCT_RNT_BTCH SET PAID_AMT=NVL(PAID_AMT,0)+ DECODE(NVL(:NEW.AC_AMTF,0),0,ABS(:NEW.AC_AMT),ABS(:NEW.AC_AMTF))
WHERE DOC_NO = :NEW.CNTRCT_NO
AND DOC_SRL = :NEW.CNTRCT_SRL
AND RNT_BATCH_NO = :NEW.CNTRCT_BTCH;
ElsIf UPDATING Then --(1)
AND DOC_SRL = :NEW.CNTRCT_
End If;--(1)
Raise_Application_Error(-20303,' Error When Update Table Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Btch . '||SqlErrm);

VIEW_PRC
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW REM_MONITION_CNTRCT
SELECT DOC_NO,DOC_SRL,DOC_DATE,CNTRCT_AMT,DAYS_CNTRCT,PAID_AMT,F_DATE_AD,T_DATE_AD,F_DATE_AH,
T_DATE_AH,FRST_PRTY,CUR_CODE,AMT_INSURNC,DAILY_RENTAL,ROUND (PAID_AMT / DAILY_RENTAL) DAILY_PIAD,
(F_DATE_AD + ROUND (PAID_AMT / DAILY_RENTAL)) T_DATE_PAID,
TO_CHAR ((F_DATE_AD + ROUND (PAID_AMT / DAILY_RENTAL)),'dd/mm/yyyy',' NLS_CALENDAR=''Arabic Hijrah''') T_DATE_PAID_AH,
SCND_PRTY,BRN_NO
FROM (SELECT MST.DOC_NO,MST.DOC_SRL,MST.DOC_DATE,CNTRCT_AMT,DAYS_CNTRCT,ROUND (SUM ( NVL (PAID_AMT, 0)+ NVL (DIS_AMT, 0)
+ NVL (CNCL_AMT, 0))) PAID_AMT,MST.F_DATE_AD,MST.T_DATE_AD,MST.F_DATE_AH,MST.T_DATE_AH,(CNTRCT_AMT / DAYS_CNTRCT) DAILY_RENTAL,
FRST_PRTY,CUR_CODE,AMT_INSURNC,SCND_PRTY,MST.BRN_NO
FROM REM_CNTRCT_RNT_BTCH BTCH, REM_CNTRCT_RNT_MST MST
WHERE MST.DOC_SRL = BTCH.DOC_SRL
GROUP BY MST.DOC_NO,MST.DOC_SRL,CNTRCT_AMT,DAYS_CNTRCT,MST.DOC_DATE,MST.F_DATE_AD,MST.T_DATE_AD,
MST.F_DATE_AH,MST.T_DATE_AH,FRST_PRTY,CUR_CODE,AMT_INSURNC,SCND_PRTY,MST.BRN_NO)
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW REM_MONITION_CNTRCT_BTCH
SELECT DOC_NO, DOC_SRL, F_DATE_AD,
T_DATE_AD, F_DATE_AH, T_DATE_AH, FRST_PRTY, CUR_CODE,BATCH_AMT,PAID_AMT, DAYS_BATCH,BATCH_DAILY_RENTAL, (PAID_AMT / BATCH_DAILY_RENTAL) BATCH_DAILY_PIAD,
DECODE ((F_DATE_AD + ROUND (PAID_AMT / BATCH_DAILY_RENTAL)) - F_DATE_AD,0, F_DATE_AD,(F_DATE_AD + ROUND (PAID_AMT / BATCH_DAILY_RENTAL))-1) T_DATE_PAID,
TO_CHAR ((F_DATE_AD + ROUND (PAID_AMT / BATCH_DAILY_RENTAL)),'dd/mm/yyyy',' NLS_CALENDAR=''Arabic Hijrah''') T_DATE_PAID_AH,
SCND_PRTY, BRN_NO, RNT_BATCH_NO
FROM (SELECT BTCH.DOC_NO,BTCH.DOC_SRL,BTCH.F_DATE_AD,BTCH.T_DATE_AD,BTCH.F_DATE_AH,BTCH.T_DATE_AH,BTCH.BATCH_AMT,ROUND (SUM ( NVL (PAID_AMT, 0)+ NVL (DIS_AMT, 0)+ NVL (CNCL_AMT, 0)),2) PAID_AMT,
(BATCH_AMT / (TO_DATE(BTCH.T_DATE_AD)-TO_DATE(BTCH.F_DATE_AD)+1)) BATCH_DAILY_RENTAL,FRST_PRTY,CUR_CODE,
(TO_DATE(BTCH.T_DATE_AD)-TO_DATE(BTCH.F_DATE_AD)+1)DAYS_BATCH,
SCND_PRTY, RNT_BATCH_NO,MST.BRN_NO
FROM REM_CNTRCT_RNT_BTCH BTCH,
REM_CNTRCT_RNT_MST MST
GROUP BY BTCH.DOC_NO,
BTCH.DOC_SRL,
BATCH_AMT,
DAYS_CNTRCT,
BTCH.F_DATE_AD,
BTCH.T_DATE_AD,
BTCH.F_DATE_AH,
BTCH.T_DATE_AH,
FRST_PRTY,
CUR_CODE,
AMT_INSURNC,
SCND_PRTY,
RNT_BATCH_NO,
MST.BRN_N
ORDER BY T_DATE_PAID ,DOC_NO
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW REM_V_CNTRCT_OCC AS
SELECT MST.DOC_SRL,REM_NO,F_DATE_AD,DECODE(NVL(CNCL_RNT,0),1,TO_DATE(CNCL_DATE,'DD/MM/YYYY'),T_DATE_AD)T_DATE_AD
FROM REM_CNTRCT_RNT_MST MST,REM_CNTRCT_RNT_DTL DTL
WHERE MST.DOC_SRL=DTL.DOC_SRL
AND NVL(RNTL_TYP,0)=2
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW REM_V_REAL_FOR_RENT AS
Select Rem_No, Rem_L_Nm, Rem_F_Nm, A_Code_Rvnu, Cc_Code, Pj_No, Actv_No,Rem_Typ, R_Code,CONN_BRN_NO
From Rem_Real_Mst
Where Nvl (Inactive, 0) = 0
And Nvl (Rem_Typ, 0) <> 9
And Not Exists (Select Rem_No
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Mst Mst, Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Dtl Dtl
Where Mst.Doc_Srl = Dtl.Doc_Srl
And Dtl.Rem_No = Rem_Real_Mst.Rem_No
Group By Rem_No
Having To_Date (Max (Decode (Nvl (Cncl_Rnt, 0),1, To_Date (Cncl_Date,'DD/MM/YYYY')-1,To_Date (T_Date_Ad, 'DD/MM/YYYY'))),
'DD/MM/YYYY') >= To_Date (Sysdate, 'DD/MM/YYYY'))
And (Nvl(Buld_No,0)) Not In
( Select Nvl(Rem_Real_Mst.Rem_No,0)
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Mst Mst,Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Dtl Dtl,Rem_Real_Mst
Group By Nvl(Rem_Real_Mst.Rem_No,0)
Having To_Date (Sysdate,'DD/MM/YYYY')
between To_Date (Max (Decode (Nvl (Cncl_Rnt, 0),1, To_Date (Cncl_Date,'DD/MM/YYYY')-1,To_Date (f_Date_Ad,'DD/MM/YYYY'))),'DD/MM/YYYY')
To_Date (Max (Decode (Nvl (Cncl_Rnt, 0),1, To_Date (Cncl_Date,'DD/MM/YYYY')-1,To_Date (T_Date_Ad,'DD/MM/YYYY'))),'DD/MM/YYYY'))
And (Nvl(Rem_No,0)) Not In
( Select Nvl(Rem_Real_Mst.Buld_No,0)
From Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Mst Mst, Rem_Cntrct_Rnt_Dtl Dtl,Rem_Real_Mst
Group By Nvl(Rem_Real_Mst.Buld_No,0)
Having To_Date (Sysdate, 'DD/MM/YYYY')
Create Or Replace Force View Rem_Tree (Code_No, Code_L_Nm,X,Aqar,Tp)
Select Code_No, Decode (1, 1, Code_L_Nm, Code_F_Nm), 0 X,0 Aqar,0 Tp
From Rem_Code_Dtl
Where Code_Typ = 3
Select To_Number(1||'000'||Rem_No),Decode (1, 1, rem_no||' - '||Rem_L_Nm, Rem_F_Nm),Nvl(Grp_No,0),Rem_No,Rem_Typ
Where Rem_Typ Not In (3, 4, 5)
Select To_Number(1||'000'||Rem_No||'000'||Flor_No),Flor_L_Nm,To_Number(1||'000'||Rem_No),0,0
From Rem_Flors
Select To_Number(9||'000'||Rem_No),Decode (1, 1, rem_no||' - '||Rem_L_Nm, Rem_F_Nm),To_Number(1||'000'||Buld_No||'000'||Flr_No),Rem_No,Rem_Typ
Where Rem_Typ In (3, 4, 5)
Create Or Replace View Real_Trans As
Select Mst.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Doc_Type_Name(1,500) Doctypname,(Select Decode(1,1,C_A_Name,C_E_Name) From Customer Where C_Code=Mst.Scnd_Prty)C_Name,Cntrct_Amt AMT,Cur_Code,Mst.Brn_No,Dtl.Rem_No
Select Mst.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Doc_Type_Name(1,502) Doctypname,(Select Decode(1,1,C_A_Name,C_E_Name) From Customer Where C_Code=Mst.Scnd_Prty)C_Name,Diff_Rvnu_Rntl Amt,Cur_Code,Mst.Brn_No,Dtl.Rem_No
Union Al
Select Mst.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Doc_Type_Name(1,501) Doctypname,(Select Decode(1,1,C_A_Name,C_E_Name) From Customer Where C_Code=Mst.Scnd_Prty)C_Name,AMT_DUE AMT,Cur_Code,Mst.Brn_No,Dtl.Rem_No
Select Mst.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Doc_Type_Name(1,504) Doctypname,(Select Decode(1,1,C_A_Name,C_E_Name) From Customer Where C_Code=Mst.Scnd_Prty)C_Name,Cntrct_Amt AMT,Cur_Code,Mst.Brn_No,Mst.Rem_No
Select Mst.Doc_No,Mst.Doc_Date,Doc_Type_Name(1,505) Doctypname,(Select Decode(1,1,C_A_Name,C_E_Name) From Customer Where C_Code=Mst.Scnd_Prty)C_Name,Cntrct_Amt AMT,Cur_Code,Mst.Brn_No,Mst.Rem_No
